# Graph Report - TCP  (2026-08-26)

## Corpus Check
- 5494 files · ~3,043,619 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 39386 nodes · 72323 edges · 2376 communities (1651 shown, 725 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 788 edges (avg confidence: 0.77)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `9ebbc46e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
